- **q-a93cd886abc378** `community_question_bank` — Semantic turn detection.** A small classifier over streaming ASR partials predicting &quot;is this complete?&quot;, used to modulate the silence threshold: roughly ~200-400 ms when it looks complete, ~1.5-2 s when it looks mid-sentence. Open-source models built for exactly this exist now, and this is the single biggest quality win available. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/10-multimodal/questions.md#L627)

- **q-2a04aea3ad99c1** `community_question_bank` — Write your LP stories first.** 8-10 STAR stories, quantified, first-person, covering at minimum: Customer Obsession, Ownership, Dive Deep, Invent and Simplify, Have Backbone/Disagree and Commit, Deliver Results, and a genuine failure. Practise them aloud - Bar Raiser follow-ups destroy stories you&#x27;ve only written down. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/amazon.md#L350)

[Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/deepseek.md#L59)

- **q-b5a86a8339760b** `community_question_bank` — What you have actually built.** Public framing and candidate reports both point the same way: portfolio and shipped work over algorithm puzzles. Bring specifics, including the failures. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/groq.md#L51)
- **q-a53b952a04090f** `community_question_bank` — Our compiler statically schedules every instruction and every chip-to-chip transfer. What does that compiler need to know that an NVCC-style compiler does not, and what breaks when it is wrong? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/groq.md#L108)
- **q-b1b2844c27728e** `community_question_bank` — Write me the host-side runtime that feeds a deterministic accelerator across many chips. What is genuinely hard about it? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/groq.md#L279)
- **q-8784225f834403** `community_question_bank` — Scale.** Does it fail at two chips? One node? One rack? The smallest failing configuration is the cheapest thing to debug, and the boundary where it first appears usually names the mechanism: failing exactly when you cross a node boundary points at the inter-node link or the deskew logic, not at the maths. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/groq.md#L307)
- **q-e69d6de95ad66f** `community_question_bank` — Why the measurement is trustworthy.** Warmup handling, run-to-run variance, whether you compared like for like, whether the win held on production shapes rather than only on the microbenchmark. Anyone can produce a favourable benchmark; interviewers here are looking for someone who tried to break their own result. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/groq.md#L328)
- **q-42baaf977e0335** `community_question_bank` — Nvidia&#x27;s Groq deal: acquisition, acquihire or creative licensing deal? - Constellation Research (deal terms, leadership change) [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/groq.md#L367)
- **q-150436de9a14de** `community_question_bank` — A new frontier model is released and it scores better on your benchmarks. What happens before it reaches customers? [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/harvey.md#L341)
- **q-b4777ef85ec6b8** `community_question_bank` — Is the clause in the parsed document at all?** Pull the extracted text and grep it. If the clause is missing, this is an ingestion bug: a scanned page that OCR mangled, a page dropped by the PDF parser, text trapped in a table or a footnote, or the clause living in an amendment that was never attached to the base agreement. In practice this is the single most common cause and the cheapest to check. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/harvey.md#L389)
- **q-f09ba171599dc2** `community_question_bank` — Did the model see it and not report it?** Only now is this a generation problem, and even then it is often a definition problem: the checklist asked for &quot;change of control&quot; and the model applied a narrower reading than the partner had in mind. Ask the partner what they expected the answer to include - the rubric may be the bug. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/harvey.md#L392)
- **q-acb0539642857e** `community_question_bank` — Compare BPE, WordPiece, and Unigram tokenization. Why did Hugging Face write `tokenizers` in Rust, and what tokenizer bugs bite people in practice? - **q-69b11ef382acf2** `community_question_bank` — Write your application like it&#x27;s the first interview round.** The recruiter&#x27;s public advice: three specific sentences on how *you* would make an impact, GitHub link included. Generic applications get skipped. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/hugging-face.md#L244)

- **q-7ce4bac8f9808f** `community_question_bank` — Communication shape.** Do collective times match theory? All-reduce moves ~2× model bytes per step; check measured bus bandwidth against NCCL benchmarks for the topology. Common failures: traffic crossing rails/spine because the scheduler placed the job across non-adjacent nodes (topology-aware placement fixes it), wrong NCCL transport config (IB vs Ethernet/RoCE settings), or PXN/NVLS features silently disabled. [Source / reading](https://github.com/ombharatiya/AI-Engineer-Interview-Questions/blob/108530e6bf6b118b23599668d8e1c9fdae054fed/14-company-interview-questions/together-ai.md#L291)

- **q-874753ccc99a9c** `community_question_bank` — Does this capability become a competitive moat? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-architect.md#L106)
- **q-7c2ee8705e7e2d** `community_question_bank` — Explainability requirement: every AI-assisted decision must have a human-reviewable rationale [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-architect.md#L156)
- **q-472db42004a66f** `community_question_bank` — What AI Engineers Do [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-engineer.md#L11)
- **q-e896687f0a6687** `community_question_bank` — Chunk granularity** — Is the chunk too large (relevant info buried in noise) or too small (missing surrounding context)? Try parent-child chunking. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-engineer.md#L124)
- **q-4d562d67d5b059** `community_question_bank` — Hallucination source** — Is the model generating from its own knowledge instead of the provided context? Add explicit instruction: &quot;Answer ONLY based on the provided documents. If the answer is not in the documents, say &#x27;I don&#x27;t have that information.&#x27;&quot; [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-engineer.md#L126)
- **q-c4561b94031841** `community_question_bank` — What AI Researchers Do [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L11)
- **q-f2bec576281e00** `community_question_bank` — Remove each component of your method independently. Does removing it hurt? By how much? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L36)
- **q-a77c0f4aa49a79** `community_question_bank` — Where does your method perform *worse* than baseline? Understanding failures is as important as successes. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L40)
- **q-e32a5e94621355** `community_question_bank` — Is the improvement uniform or concentrated in specific examples? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L41)
- **q-01f0bf7e3b9752** `community_question_bank` — Does your method use the same compute as the baseline? Improvements that require 2× compute should be compared against baselines with the same 2× compute budget. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L44)
- **q-85134f7c1795b1** `community_question_bank` — Scientific importance**: does this advance fundamental understanding? Does it resolve an open theoretical question? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L56)
- **q-59e546c3e1ebff** `community_question_bank` — Timing**: is the field ready for this? (1) necessary prerequisites exist, (2) compute is available, (3) enough researchers are adjacent to build on your work. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L60)
- **q-fbc263b0ced2e4** `community_question_bank` — Tractability**: can you make meaningful progress in a reasonable timeframe? Long-shot moonshots are valuable, but a portfolio of research should include tractable near-term wins. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L62)
- **q-50a36cf82d231c** `community_question_bank` — Scaling behavior questions — how does X scale with compute, data, or model size? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L68)
- **q-88fbf3e715a014** `community_question_bank` — Hyperparameter sensitivity**: did you tune both baseline and proposed method equally? An untuned baseline is an unfair comparison. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L86)
- **q-fb945cc1a34a1f** `community_question_bank` — Scale dependence**: does your method require more data, compute, or model size to show improvement? Test at different scales. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L90)
- **q-21584024c0e700** `community_question_bank` — Pivot strategically**: what did you learn that opens new directions? Failed experiments often reveal surprising properties of the problem that suggest better approaches. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L98)
- **q-86f359ce201062** `community_question_bank` — Compute budget first**: what&#x27;s your FLOPs budget? Compute ≈ 6 × N × D. [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L119)
- **q-d78974eaaa0597** `community_question_bank` — Quantify the distribution shift — feature-level vs. label-level vs. both? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L190)
- **q-402835abab726b** `community_question_bank` — Is shift covariate (same labels, different features), label (different label distribution), or concept drift (feature-label relationship changed)? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L191)
- **q-7ef06cfbc6f0a9** `community_question_bank` — Which features or patterns are most affected? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/ai-researcher.md#L192)

- **q-a6271cc55090b9** `community_question_bank` — Edit distance: how much do users modify AI suggestions? (lower = higher quality OR lower agency) [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/data-scientist.md#L65)

- **q-2f2486e26dd093** `community_question_bank` — 3% accuracy reduction: what does this translate to in business terms? [Source / reading](https://github.com/JustInCache/awesome-ai-interviews/blob/61d1d99635ef9cbd97f57fee7a03a8393df561e0/roles/data-scientist.md#L117)
